  window.hashRestrictionIdentifier = function(id) {
    var hash = 0;
    var str = id+"";
    if (str.length == 0) return hash;
    for (i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      char = str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = ((hash<<5)-hash)+char;
      hash = hash & hash; // Convert to 32bit integer
    }
    return hash;
  }
  window.maybeLoadRestrictedScript = function(script) {
    var delay = 0;
    var show_on = 'all';
    var limit = '';
    var disabled = false;
    var identifier = '';
    var hide_on = ''
    var cookie = {
      get: function(cname) {
        var name = cname + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for(var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
                c = c.substring(1);
            }
            if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
                return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
            }
        }
        return "";
      },
      set: function(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
        var d = new Date();
        d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        var expires = "expires="+d.toUTCString();
        document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
      }
    }
    var is_home = true;
    for(var i=0; i<window.restrictedScriptsConfig.length; ++i) {
      var restriction = window.restrictedScriptsConfig[i];

      if (!restriction.restriction_enabled || script.indexOf(restriction.identifier) === -1) {
        continue;
      }
      delay = restriction.delay;
      disabled = restriction.disabled;
      limit = restriction.limit + '';
      show_on = restriction.show_on;
      identifier = restriction.identifier;
      hide_on = restriction.hide_on;
      hide_on_regex = new RegExp(hide_on, 'i')
    }
    var device = $(window).width() > 1024 ? 'desktop' : 'mobile';
    if (disabled || (is_home && show_on == 'not_hp') || (!is_home && show_on == 'hp') || (hide_on && hide_on.length && hide_on.length > 0 && hide_on_regex.test(device + ':' + window.location))) {
      // console.log('Canceling the load of', script)
      return;
    }
    var timesLoaded = 0;
    var cookieName = null;
    if(identifier != '') {
      cookieName = 'restriction_' + window.hashRestrictionIdentifier(identifier).toString(32);
    }
    if(cookieName != null) {
      timesLoaded = cookie.get(cookieName);
      if(timesLoaded == "") {
        timesLoaded = "0";
      }
      timesLoaded = timesLoaded * 1;
    }
    if(limit != '') {
      limit = limit * 1;
      if(limit <= timesLoaded) {
        return;
      }
    }
    setTimeout(function(){
      var s = document.createElement('script');
      s.type = 'text/javascript';
      s.async = true;
      s.src = script;
      var x = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
      x.parentNode.insertBefore(s, x);
      if(cookieName != null) {
        cookie.set(cookieName, timesLoaded+1, 30);
      } 
    }, delay * 1000);
  }

                            "first_name": window.customerData ? window.customerData.first_name : null,
                            "last_name": window.customerData ? window.customerData.last_name : null
                          }
                      });
                  </script>
</ul>

class=\"cSizeChartStep-title\"\u003eBand Size (Ribcage measurement)\u003c\/div\u003e\n                \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-text\"\u003e\n                  Measure snugly around your ribcage, directly under your bust. Add 2” to your underbust measurement,\n                  \u0026amp; round up to the closest even number.\n                \u003c\/div\u003e\n                \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-example\"\u003e\n                  31” + 2” = 33”, rounded up to 34” Band size = 34”\n                \u003c\/div\u003e\n              \u003c\/div\u003e\n            \u003c\/div\u003e\n\n            \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep\"\u003e\n              \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-image cSizeChartStep-image--last\"\u003e\u003c\/div\u003e\n              \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-content\"\u003e\n\n                \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-title\"\u003eCup Size (Full bust measurement) \u003c\/div\u003e\n                \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-text\"\u003e\n                  Wrap the measuring tape snugly around the fullest part of your bust. Compare yourfull bust measurement to the ribcage measurement that you took (please use the initial measurement result before adding two inches or rounding up to the nearest even number). For every inch difference, add a cup size.\n                \u003c\/div\u003e\n                \u003cdiv class=\"cSizeChartStep-example\"\u003e\n                  31” ribcage measurement \u0026amp; 36” across-bust measurement = 5” difference \u003cbr \/\u003e\n                  Go up 5 cup sizes.
